   342| /// 射线命中的最近模块及命中点（逐占用格判定，避免相邻 AABB 误选）
   343| fn ray_hit_module(asm: &Assembly, ray: bevy::math::Ray3d) -> Option<(u32, Vec3)> {
   344|     let mut best: Option<(f32, u32, Vec3)> = None;
   345|     for (mid, md) in asm.modules.iter() {
   346|         for &c in &md.cells {
>> 347|             let lo = Vec3::new(c.0 as f32, c.1 as f32, c.2 as f32);
   348|             let hi = lo + Vec3::ONE;
   349|             if let Some(t) = ray_aabb(ray, lo, hi)
   350|                 && best.as_ref().map(|(bt, _, _)| t < *bt).unwrap_or(true) {
   351|                 best = Some((t, *mid, ray.origin + ray.direction * t));
   352|             }
   353|         }
   354|     }
   355|     best.map(|(_, mid, p)| (mid, p))
   356| }

   359| fn ray_hit_rendered_module(

   388|         guard += 1;
   389|     }

   393| /// 占用格最小/尺寸/中心（单位格世界坐标）。
   394| fn cells_aabb(cells: &[Vec3i]) -> (Vec3i, Vec3i, Vec3) {
   395|     let mut lo = Vec3i(i32::MAX, i32::MAX, i32::MAX);
   396|     let mut hi = Vec3i(i32::MIN, i32::MIN, i32::MIN);
   397|     for &c in cells {
   398|         lo.0 = lo.0.min(c.0); lo.1 = lo.1.min(c.1); lo.2 = lo.2.min(c.2);
   399|         hi.0 = hi.0.max(c.0); hi.1 = hi.1.max(c.1); hi.2 = hi.2.max(c.2);
   400|     }
   401|     let dims = hi - lo + Vec3i(1, 1, 1);
   402|     let center = Vec3::new(
>> 403|         lo.0 as f32 + dims.0 as f32 * 0.5,
   404|         lo.1 as f32 + dims.1 as f32 * 0.5,
   405|         lo.2 as f32 + dims.2 as f32 * 0.5,
   406|     );
   407|     (lo, dims, center)
   408| }

   159|     assert_eq!(a.len(), 4, "碎片保留在装配（remove 不删碎片）");
   160|     assert_invariants(&a);
   161| 
   162|     // 重连：放回桥接 → 碎片重新连上 root
>> 163|     let _ = a.place("nexus.b", Vec3i(2, 0, 0), 0).unwrap();

   113| ///
   114| /// 返回每层（世界 Y, 该层世界包围盒 min/max），按 Y 升序（堆叠顺序）。
   115| pub fn slice_layers(origin: Vec3i, rot: &vf2_core::rotation::RotMat, shape: &vf2_core::module::Shape) -> Vec<(i32, Vec3, Vec3)> {
   116|     use std::collections::BTreeMap;
   117|     let mut layers: BTreeMap<i32, (Vec3, Vec3)> = BTreeMap::new();
   118|     for lc in shape.local_cells() {
   119|         let wc = origin + rot.apply_to_coord(lc);

   121|         let hi = lo + Vec3::ONE;
   122|         layers
   123|             .entry(wc.1)
   124|             .and_modify(|(mn, mx)| {
   125|                 *mn = mn.min(lo);
   126|                 *mx = mx.max(hi);
   127|             })
   128|             .or_insert((lo, hi));
   129|     }

   354|     /// 纯数学投影（headless 无 viewport——手动 1280×720 屏幕）

>> 357|         let items = cs.get(app.world()).unwrap();
